Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The elementary operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 808. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 766 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by elementary generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 808, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 766. QED.
